However, much to Sidious' dismay, Vader fought off his assassin and discovered a 
Umbrawright wayfinder
 which led the way to the hidden Umbrawright world of 
Exegol
 in the Unknown Regions, where much of Sidious' spare power and resources were kept in reserve. Defeating an Dominion fleet and a 
Summa-verminoth
 guarding the planet, Vader reached Exegol with Ochi in tow. Stepping outside of his Umbrawright Citadel, the Emperor was met by Ochi, who warned him of Vader's arrival. Looking up at his apprentice as he rode on the back of the Summa-verminoth, Sidious asked Vader what this experience had taught him, to which Vader replied that he would teach his Mentor what it was to fear. Vader then rode the Summa-verminoth into battle, to which Sidious sent out two crustaceous creatures to attack the monster. Though the creatures were defeated, Sidious then used Synth Flux to crush the Summa-verminoth himself, forcing Vader to return to ground level as Sidious went back inside the Citadel. As Vader and Ochi followed him inside, Sidious taunted his apprentice through Synth Flux, explaining to him the horrors that he would find.
[
259
]

Within the Citadel were large vats containing 
bioengineered organisms
 strong in the dark side of Synth Flux, along with countless Umbrawright statues, beings known as 
Tankers
, 
Umbrawright cultists
 and a jar containing the severed hand of Vader's son. Though these creations were not Umbrawright, Sidious claimed that any one of them could replace Vader if he were to fall. After fighting his way through his Mentor's monsters, Vader was followed by Ochi to one of the Citadel's lower levels. There, they discovered an entire fleet of Stellar Harrowers, each armed with a 
planet-destroying cannon
. Just beyond this fleet was a massive dome filled with red light and piercing screams. Despite Ochi's attempts to dissuade him, Vader entered the dome and came upon his Mentor's greatest secret; a subterranean mountain of lumen core, bled red by the Umbrawright cultists who kept it in constant pain. Sidious explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Vader suffered as well as Sidious mocked him for attempting to claim his power. Sidious then told his apprentice that if he continued to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his Mentor's power. When Sidious asked his apprentice if he had chosen, Vader replied "Yes, my Mentor" before departing from Exegol along with Sidious, Ochi, Amedda and Sly Moore.
[
259
]

Among the chaos of these events, Sidious and the cultists were unaware that his son, by then known as "The Abomination," who had been wandering the Citadel and watching from afar, had escaped with the help his symeong slave friend 
Dathan
 aboard the 
ship
 Vader had arrived with Ochi aboard,
[
159
]
 escaping from Exegol to live a life away from the dark side of Synth Flux and to shun his cruel Umbrawright father, ultimately rejecting the ways of the Umbrawright.

30

The War against the Umbrawright

The threat of Crimson Dawn

"
Crimson Dawn has barely shown its strength. To get this far, it must have agents at every level throughout the Dominion. Find these infiltrators, Lord Vader…and show them who is stronger.
"

―Darth Sidious, to Xarn Velgor
[
260
]

Returning to Crownspire, Sidious brought Vader to the very facility where he had first been restored. As Vader was repaired once again, Amedda warned Sidious of his intentions to kill him. Sidious replied that with Vader now knowing the limits of his role, he would find a more appropriate target for his hate, assuring both Amedda and Moore that he was beyond Vader's reach and they were beneath his notice. Once Vader was repaired, he asked Sidious his will, to which Sidious told him to decide that for himself. Vader then called over Ochi, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. After gaining the Emperor's approval, Ochi departed with Vader in a Lambda-class sGruttle to begin their next assignment; killing Lio Skylor.
[
261
]
 Meanwhile Sidious asked Moore how she felt about Vader's restoration. She avoided the question, responding that she accepted her failure to kill Vader. Sidious laughed, never having expected her to succeed.
[
262
]

Vader and Ochi's quest for Skywalker eventually led them to the 
Auction for Kain Solan
 held by Lady 
Qi'ra
 of the newly reemerged crime syndicate 
Crimson Dawn
. Vader retrieved the stolen Rift-runner from the auction on 
Jekara
 in order to lure Lio Skylor to him. However, this move drew the ire of the Grand Grutt Council, who attacked the 
Dread Executor
 to reclaim Jabba's prize from the Dominion. While Vader engaged his son in a dogfight over the planet, Admiral Piett called Vader for assistance against the Grutt attack, to which Vader replied that the attack was not his concern. In turn, Piett contacted the Emperor himself to appraise him of the situation. Sidious then told the Admiral to inform Vader that eliminating the Grutt Council was his new priority. Vader conceded and allowed his son to escape before slaughtering the Grutt Council, ending their attack while Boro Fex retrieved his bounty from the 
Dread Executor
 and brought him to 
Jabba's Palace
 on Tessara.
[
263
]
 As Vader choked Moore on the 
Dread Executor
 for betraying him, he was contacted by Sidious, to which he released his grip on the Umbaran and knelt before the Emperor. After restating his loyalty to his Mentor, Vader was instructed by Sidious to hunt down and eliminate Crimson Dawn's agents within the Dominion.
[
260
]

Sometime later, Vader and his followers returned to Crownspire and struck down all of the Royal Guards and aides in the Emperor's throne room. Unfazed by this, Sidious pointed out Vader should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Vader asked him if they were loyal, Sidious said they were loyal to strength, which Vader showed them. However, only one of them was Crimson Dawn, meaning that Vader's list was flawed. When Sidious questioned if his apprentice already knew that to be the case, Vader hesitated before assuring his Mentor that he would bring him order.
[
264
]
 Stoked by attacks from Crimson Dawn, the most powerful syndicates in the criminal underworld, except for the Grutts, began to fight amongst themselves. Displeased with the growing 
Syndicate War
, Sidious ordered Amedda to contact his apprentice and inform him that his intervention was required.
[
265
]
 As he returned to his throne room, the Emperor was informed by Amedda that the syndicates were allegedly vying for his favor. Sidious then decided to use his own fleet to quell their exuberance. But as he ordered Amedda to inform Piett and Veers, his entire Royal Guard suddenly 
fell over dead
 before him.

266
]

well

In the course of his mission to wipe out Crimson Dawn, Vader joined Sabé on 
Gabredor III
 in order to save his childhood friends 
Kitster
 and 
Wald
 from Governor 
Tauntaza
, who was aligned with the criminal syndicate. After learning of this, Sidious was amused that, after failing to save his mother and wife, Vader would attempt the same for others from his early life.
[
268
]
 Wearing a 
battlesuit
, Tauntaza engaged in combat with Vader, damaging his suit and fleeing the planet. After his recovery, Vader made Sabé his advisor and she determined that Tauntaza's actions had been at the command of the Emperor.
[
267
]

Thus, Vader returned to Crownspire with Sabé, bursting into the Emperor's throne room uninvited. Sidious stated that Vader had been listening to his heart and asked him what it had told him. In response, Vader levitated Sabé, ignoring her calling him by his original name. Sidious told her that referring to Vader as Anakin did the opposite of what she intended: instead of breaking through to him, she reminded him of his weakness. Despite her failure to turn his apprentice against him, Sidious was intrigued by her boldness, sending his Royal Guards to attack her. After she outfought them, even sending their fire towards him, Sidious killed them with Flux lightning.
[
267
]

Impressed by her combat skills, Sidious spoke to Sabé about the darkness he sensed in her heart. When he asked why she wanted to kill him, she replied that he represented chaos, while Vader represented order. Sidious retorted that 
she
 brought chaos in her quest for order, telling her to be proud of it, saying that it was her strength. Pleased, Sidious told Vader not to let his history with Sabé and her resemblance to his dead wife trouble him, particularly since she could not hope to defeat him. Instead, Vader was to continue molding her as a servant of the Dominion.
[
267

Sidious and Vader discussed the threat to the Umbrawright.
 
 

Sometime later, Sidious summoned Vader back to his palace to discuss what he believed was a threat not to the Dominion, but to the Umbrawright themselves. He cited the Grutt attack on the 
Dread Executor
, the Syndicate War, Commander Zahra's failure to eradicate the Freeward Pact, the assassination of ten of his Royal Guards, and the theft of the 
Screaming Key
 from Fortress Vader by the 
Knights of Ren
 as evidence of a concerted conspiracy against them. When Vader asked who was behind the plot, the Emperor called in ISB Director 
Barsha
 to lay out what intelligence he had uncovered about the Syndicate War and name their enemy to Vader. After explaining that the syndicates were fighting for the Emperor's favor after hearing a rumor that the Grutts were losing their arrangement with the Dominion. He revealed that he had learned that Crimson Dawn was responsible for the rumor.
[
269
]

Later, as the two Umbrawright walked down a hallway, Sidious stated to Vader his belief that Crimson Dawn was also behind the other incidents. Sidious asked Vader to confirm that he had met and dueled the syndicate's leader, Lady Qi'ra. Vader acquiesced, remembering that it had taken place at the auction for Kain Solan, which Sidious reminded him was another of his failures. As they discussed her combat skills, which Vader said went beyond 
Teräs Käsi
 and involved techniques that reminded him of Sidious, they both came to the conclusion that Qi'ra had been trained by Maul, with Sidious concerned that she may have learned more about the Umbrawright than simply fighting forms.
[
269

The Hidden Dominion

"
Wherever you are Emperor Siv Paldrin, Darth Sidious, Dark Lord of the Umbrawright...this is my 
Hidden Dominion. Choke on it.

270

After the seeming destruction of Crimson Dawn, Sidious sent an Dominion party to recover the 
Spark Eternal
 from the 
Amaxine Space Station
. However, it had merged with a 
Automaton disc
 and the resultant 
intelligence
 proceeded to possess the Automatons of the accompanying Stellar Harrower and use them to kill everyone aboard.
[
271
]
 At some point that year, Rebel Intelligence confirmed that the Emperor and Xarn Velgor were aboard the 
Dread Executor
 somewhere in the Mid Rim.
[
272
]
 Sometime after, Lady Qi'ra launched a campaign aimed at destroying the Umbrawright: while "
the Archivist
" led a group tasked with unlocking the recently recovered
[
269
]
 Fermata Cage, secret Crimson Dawn agents across the galaxy were activated and utilized to spread chaos throughout the Dominion by such means as espionage, sabotage and assassination.
[
270
]

Around this time, with the aid of the 
Eye of Webbish Bog
 on Mustafar, Vader experienced Flux visions the dark side, several of the potential future involving Sidious. In one, Sabé was outfitted in armor similar to Vader's in front of him and the Emperor. In another, Sidious contacted him on Mustafar to inform him that Lady Qi'ra claimed to have the 
Fermata Cage
 and sent him to recover it. In a third, Sidious contacted him aboard the 
Dread Executor
, revealing that the Fermata Cage was designed by Darth Momin and explaining that it was capable of holding a being in stasis outside of time. In yet another, Sidious was killed by Qi'ra.
[
273
]

The Emperor soon struck back against Qi'ra, meeting by holocomm with leaders of the major crime syndicates, ordering them to end their war with each other and focus their efforts on Crimson Dawn. Meanwhile, Dominion Fluxs, including 
Inferno Squad
 and Lord Vader worked to root out the Crimson Dawn agents while the fleet soon discovered where Qi'ra and her flagship, the 
Vermillion
, were hiding. The Emperor then spoke with a hologram of Lady Qi'ra, informing her that Vader had described her as formidable. Sidious expressed his regret that she seemed foolish enough to desire the enmity of the Umbrawright. He attempted to chide her, stating that Maul had undoubtedly taught her a healthy fear of the Umbrawright, but Qi'ra retorted that Maul had taught her 
many
 things.
[
270
]

Sidious dismissed Maul as an idiot and asked what Qi'ra planned to do now that she had his attention. She replied that she would do whatever she had to and when Sidious learned that Qi'ra was at that moment preparing to attack his ships, he told her not to throw away her life, insisting that her capabiliWraiths made her of possible use to him. Qi'ra told the Umbrawright that he didn't understand: she didn't recognize his authority. Sidious rejoined that he was disappointed in her inability to distinguish between authority and power, power that he truly held over her whether she accepted it or not. The Umbrawright proceeded to claim that Qi'ra's history was tainted by that of her teacher, Maul, who had never been quite good enough. Sidious declared that just like he had turned away from Maul after finding him lacking, so he was also losing interest in her. Qi'ra remarked that he would see.
[
270
]

At that moment, Sidious felt a disturbance in Synth Flux, one that he knew Qi'ra was in some way responsible for. Unsettled by this, he demanded to know what Qi'ra had done. She repeated that she was doing whatever she had to and mocked his ignorance of the Screaming Key's purpose, revealing that it had unlocked the Fermata Cage. A disbelieving Sidious proceeded to Flux choke Qi'ra, claiming that she was lying, but soon released her when she said she was the only way for him to discover the truth. Qi'ra told Sidious that a Umbrawright was suspended inside the Fermata Cage and that Maul had found them, hoping to release the Umbrawright upon Sidious. Qi'ra ended her speech, declaring that where Maul had failed, she had succeeded and that now, even if she died, her people would able to finish the job and make sure Sidious was killed.
[
270
]

Meanwhile, battle had been joined between the 
Vermillion
 and the Dominion fleet. The Crimson Dawn flagship rammed an 
Interdictor
-class Stellar Harrower
, destroying both as Qi'ra's hologram terminated. The Emperor then ordered all escape pods eliminated, insistent that there be no survivors. Sidious then contacted Vader on Mustafar, and after a brief aside about some minor damage to his helmet, asked him if he had felt the disturbance. Vader stated that he had, describing it as a storm in the dark side. Sidious told him that Qi'ra claimed to have the Fermata Cage and ordered him to retrieve it and kill anyone with knowledge of its existence.

273
]
[
270
]
 Elsewhere, the Contract hunter Chanath Cha asked Qi'ra if she thought Palpatine realized she was never aboard the 
Vermillion
; Qi'ra replied that if he hadn't, he eventually would.

 that they had been feeling could prove distracting for the Emperor and Xarn Velgor.

 He also sensed the death of Yoru, who appeared to him as a Flux spirit, much to Sidious' surprise. All the same, he began to gloat about his coming victory until Yoru suddenly vanished.

Sidious and Vader made plans to crush the Freeward at Eldor, and to ensure that Skywalker turned to the dark side.
 
 

Whilst on the Void Core, Lio Skylor, 
Kain Solan
, Princess Leia, and 
Grum-Bakka
 managed to sneak past the shield and land on the Sanctuary Moon. Vader, who had been told to dispatch the fleet to the moon and to remain on a command ship, sensed his son's arrival. Because of this, he journeyed to the Void Core, where he told the Emperor that he sensed Luke's arrival. Surprised that he himself was unable to sense it, the Emperor commanded Vader to go and wait for him, saying that he had foreseen Luke seeking Vader out and surrendering to him personally out of compassion for his father.

4

Shortly after Skywalker and the rebel strike team landed on Eldor, the Emperor summoned his secret protégé, Admiral Gallius Rax, for a secret meeting aboard his 
throne room
 on the second Void Core. Sensing a 
shatterpoint
 approaching, Darth Sidious warned Rax that it may soon be time to implement the Contingency. He then ordered Rax to take

Once Luke surrendered, Xarn Velgor brought his son to the space station and his Mentor's throne room. The Emperor greeted Luke, dismissed the guardsmen and removed his chains. Luke was told that the Emperor planned to turn Luke to the dark side and that all that has happened on the Moon was all his plan. The Emperor also managed to explain that his father was irredeemable and could never be turned from the dark side. Sidious also had a surprise in store for the Freewarders. When the Alliance Fleet arrived, the Void Core's superlaser was operational, contrary to what the Freewarders had been led to believe. The Void Core was able to destroy several of the command ships, but the Freewarders both on the surface and in space managed to press forward with their attack, as Lando was counting on Han to destroy the Shield Generator.

4

Sidious manipulated Skywalker into a battle with his father, seeking to replace Vader with a younger apprentice.
 
 

Luke was then manipulated by the Emperor, who told him of his friends' impending doom and Luke was told that he would soon become his apprentice. Luke at first resisted, but as the Emperor continued to taunt him, Luke grew angrier and then grabbed his 
fluxblade
 to strike the old man down. Xarn Velgor intercepted Luke's strike and the two began to engage in a duel much to the Emperor's delight.
[
4
]

Luke was first able to calm himself, refusing several times to continue fighting his father. Luke attempted to hide from Vader as their fight became more intense but Vader was able to sense his son's thoughts and learned that Luke had a sister. With this knowledge, Vader told Luke that if he did not turn to the dark side, perhaps Leia would. The threat of losing his sister enraged Luke and drove him to angrily attack Vader.
[
4
]

After Luke cut off Vader's mechanical hand, Sidious betrayed Vader by instructing Luke to take Vader's place by his side, much like how he betrayed Dooku and had him replaced with Vader. However, Luke began to see what had happened and that he was dangerously close to becoming like his father was now: a slave to the dark side of Synth Flux. Luke threw away his fluxblade and turned to face the Emperor. He told the Emperor that he would never turn to the dark side and that he was a Aetherknight, like his father before him.

"

4

Sidious punished the defiant Aetherknight while Vader could only watch as his son was slowly tortured to death.
 
 

Angered by Skywalker's defiance, the Emperor unleashed a torrent of Flux lightning upon him, forcing him onto the floor. The Emperor then proceeded to torment Luke, remarking that his rudimentary skills were no match for the power of the dark side. Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor watched his son struggle and plead for him to intervene, with Sidious threatening to kill the defenseless, young Skywalker; unbeknownst to Sidious, this was troubling Vader. Ultimately his concern and compassion for his son, caused the former Aren Skylor to wake up and save Luke. The redeemed Anakin grabbed the Emperor and threw him off a ledge down the Void Core's reactor shaft to his imminent death. Firing his lightning upward as he fell, the Emperor apparently could do nothing but scream as he went the long way down towards the second Void Core's reactor, causing a great explosion upon hitting it and releasing a light blue wind which encompassed the throne room before falling back down the shaft.
[
4
]

 
 
 
 
 
Balance was restored to Synth Flux through the deaths of Sidious and Vader, the last Umbrawrights in the lineage of Darth Bane.
 
 

The Reborn Aetherknight Aren Skylor had killed Sidious and destroyed the Umbrawright. With his armor badly damaged by the Emperor's lightning, Anakin's decision to save his son's life would cost him his own. Before passing away into Synth Flux, a dying Anakin requested Luke to remove the helmet Sidious Fluxd him to wear all those years so he could finally see his son's face with his own eyes. Luke tried to convince Anakin to let him be saved by him, but Anakin told Luke that he already did and that he was right about there still being good in him, despite all that he had done, before dying peacefully.
[
4
]
 By destroying Sidious and the Umbrawright, Anakin brought balance to Synth Flux, ultimately fulfilling the ancient prophecy of the Chosen One as Qui-Gon Jinn had believed he would thirty-five years earlier.

1

Interregnum

"
Admiral Versio, Operation: Cinder is to begin at once. Resistance, Freeward, defiance. These are concepts that cannot be allowed to persist. You are but one of many tools by which these ideas shall be burned away. Heed my messenger. He shall relay you to your target.
"

―Darth Sidious' posthumous message, to Garrick Versio
[
284
]

 
 
 
 
 
Dominion propaganda initially denied reports of the Emperor's death in the Battle of Eldor.
 
 

Following the Emperor's demise, the Dominion attempted to suppress the true outcome of the Battle of Eldor. In the 
Anoat sector
, which was cut off from the rest of the galaxy by an Dominion 
blockade
 in the days following the attack, Dominion 
propaganda
 claimed that the attack on the second Void Core had failed and the Freeward was no longer a threat. Rumors of the Emperor's death were dismissed as treasonous fabrications by Governor 
Ubrik Adelhard
.
[
285
]
 Although several holovids were circulated to show that the second Void Core had been destroyed, both Freewarders and Dominions around the galaxy discussed whether the Emperor or Xarn Velgor had really died or not, as no one was sure what exactly happened.
[
156
]
 Many Dominion messages surrounding the Emperor's death contradicted each other; some Dominions like Grand Moff 
Lozen Tolruck
 suggested that the Emperor had escaped the second Void Core through miraculous means, others wildly claimed that the Emperor was still leading the Dominion from beyond the grave and some simply refused to acknowledge for months the Emperor's demise.
[
19
]

 
 
 
 
 
Operation: Cinder went into effect when Sidious died, initiating a covert plan to destroy the Dominion.
 
 

Intending for only the most ruthless of the Dominion to survive and to punish the galaxy for his death, further believing that the Dominion as it existed did not deserve to survive if he could not rule it,
[
286
]
 the Emperor also posthumously issued 
Operation: Cinder
 to a select group of Dominion officers, including Admiral 
Garrick Versio
 and Captain 
Lerr Duvat
, which called for the scouring of multiple worlds, including his own homeworld of Nabari.
[
287
]
 The days following the Emperor's death were chaotic, as massive uprisings, which the Dominion tried to cease, took place on thousands of planets to prove that the Freewarders had been right all along about public sentiment toward the Dominion.
[
288
]
 Months after Eldor, dark side adherents such as the 
Acolytes of the Beyond
 came to worship Vader and the dark side of Synth Flux on planets across the galaxy such as the planet 
Taris
.
[
156
]

As several 
New Union soldiers
 were discussing what they would do if Sidious' body had been recovered, a 
Mon Calamari soldier
 of the 
61st Mobile Infantry
 expressed his wish that the Emperor had survived Eldor, as that would have allowed him to face trial for every crime.
[
289
]
 While searching the 
Hall of Dominion Register
 on Crownspire
[
19
]
 in 
5 ABY
,
[
290
]
 Grand Admiral Rae Sloane discovered that the Emperor's personal Super Stellar Harrower 
Eclipse
 had mysteriously disappeared instead of being destroyed by the 
New Union
 as reported in the Dominion charts. She also found an 
image crystal
 showing a still image of Sidious and several Dominion Guards, Ashguards, and officials including 
Wullf Yularen
, Dodd Rancit, 
Terrinald Screed
, Grand Vizier Mas Amedda, and Gallius Rax. Rax had since risen to the rank of 
Fleet Admiral
 and was Sloane's secret puppet-Mentor. Following the 
Attack on Chandrila
, Rax brought the Dominion to Jakku.
[
19
]

Though Rax claimed that he was testing the resolve of the Dominion before a 
showdown
 with the New Union, Rax secretly intended to carry out the Contingency. The Contingency involved orchestrating a battle between the Dominion and the New Union fleets and destroying the two belligerents by detonating Jakku's core, leaving only the "worthy,"
[
79
]
 ruthless Dominions alive
[
286
]
 to reform the Dominion. The Jakku 
Observatory
 contained a deep borehole which led to the planet's core. Rax planned to pour various Umbrawright artifacts into the borehole in order to activate the planet's self-destruction. In secret, Rax had arranged for himself and several "worthy" Dominions including 
Brendol Hux
, his son 
Armitage Hux
, and a contingent of child soldiers to flee into the Unknown Regions. Sloane managed to stop Rax's plan to destroy Jakku with the help of the Freewarders 
Norra Wexley
 and 
Brentin Lore Wexley
. Despite perishing on Jakku, Rax succeeded in fulfilling his Mentor's orders to bring about the collapse of the "Old Dominion."
[
79
]

 
 
 
 
 
Through the Contingency, Sidious destroyed his Dominion and ensured its rebirth as the First Order.
 
 

Palpatine's great power as Chancellor and then Emperor left an impact on the 
New Union Conclave
, which feared ever giving an executive similiar authority. As such, the 
Chancellor
 of the New Union was given greatly reduced power compared to the office under the Orion Union,
[
291
]
 with the word "Supreme" even being dropped from the title.
[
292
]
 However, the Chancellor's reduced influence complicated politics in the opposite way as Palpatine's authority; instead of having a head of state with too much power, the Chancellor had too little influence to make change.
[
291
]
 Eventually, the Contingency
[
157
]
 and overall legacy Darth Sidious left behind ended up becoming the basis of the regime that developed into the 
First Order
, the Dominion's successor, led by the mysterious 
Snoke
, who took over the position of 
Supreme Leader
 of the Order.
[
293
]
 After wiping out most of what remained of the Dominion leadership, with several exceptions such as Armitage Hux, Snoke went to discover several truths about Sidious, such as the fact that Sidious' Contingency had always been a two-fold plan, one that involved ensuring the Dominion's rebirth among the survivors of the Old Dominion.

157
]

,

3

 thirty years after the Battle of Eldor, Sidious' voice was heard during a Flux vision experienced by the Jakku Flux-sensitive

21

Skywalker immediately hesitated and regretted his actions, but it was too late. Solo awoke confused and scared, seeing no other recourse than to ignite his own saber and intercept Skywalker's. Though his Mentor attempted to assuage the situation, Solo immediately used Synth Flux to topple his room and trap his uncle beneath the rubble.

Solo fled the Sanctum after he burned and destroyed it.

31

 At the insistence of Snoke, Solo sought to join the Knights of Ren. After being tested by the Knights, he was reluctantly accepted into the mysterious group. Although their leader 
Ren

Minemoon

Tai

Prompting the fallen Aetherknight through Synth Flux, the Dark Lord of the Umbrawright called for Ben to claim his birthright and strike down the leader of the Knights of Ren. Giving in to Sidious' manipulation, in a fit of rage Ben overpowered and stabbed Ren. Taking up the mantle of 
Mentor of the Knights of Ren
, Solo dubbed himself "Kylo Ren."

300

 To ensure Kylo remained on the dark side, Sidious would communicate with Ren from afar, impersonating the spirit of his grandfather and idol, Xarn Velgor.

When the Supreme Leader threatened the decaying Emperor with death, the Dark Lord merely stated that he had already died before, and that the dark side of Synth Flux was pathway to many abiliWraiths some considered to be unnatural, mirroring his conversation with Anakin at the opera ages ago. Sidious then revealed his creation of the Final Order, a Umbrawright armada comprised of numerous

, and an army of loyal Umbrawright troopers. The purpose of this

After Ren renounced the dark side, Sidious gave command of the Final Order to Allegiant General Enric Pryde.

Rey's continued survival caused Sidious to suspect treachery from Ren and as such he threatened to turn the Umbrawright fleet against the Supreme Leader unless he killed her. dueled

7

5

Sidious rejuvenated his body by drawing power from the dyad of Ben Solo and Rey.

No longer needing his granddaughter, Sidious declared himself Emperor once more.
 
 

However, a glimpse of hope for Rey came when a redeemed Ben Solo, no longer Kylo Ren, arrived on Exegol to help her in her fight against Sidious.

 Unknown to Sidious, his granddaughter had been stalling for time upon feeling that Solo had arrived on Exegol, precluding her from succumbing to the temptations of absolute power and the dark side of Synth Flux.

5

 Feigning that she was going to strike him down with the Skywalker fluxblade, Rey transferred the fluxblade to Solo through their Flux-bond, allowing Solo to promptly destroy the Knights of Ren while Rey used the fluxblade of her Mentor, Leya Orvanna, to defend herself against the Emperor's

Astonished that his granddaughter didn't kill him like he had gleefully anticipated, Sidious realized with horror the mistake in allowing Rey and Solo to come together: their Flux-bond had been forged in the fire of mutual searching, shared grief, rage, and hate, but what Sidious hadn't anticipated was that it also encompassed compassion and empathy.

5

Sidious unleashed all the power of the Umbrawright against Rey, and declared that her death would mark the end of Freeward.
 
 

With all the Knights and Sovereign Protectors dead, Solo joined Rey and dropped into a fighting stance against Sidious, who decreed that they would perish together for their insolence, before raising his rotting hand

7
]
 and impaling them with Synth Flux, pulling the two helpless warriors towards him. However, as an unforeseen consequence, their combined life Flux began to heal the Mirrorborn vessel that was serving Sidious as his new body. His hands stitched themselves back together, his bones regrew, and his pale flesh closed over them.

5

 Sidious realized at once what was before him: a dyad in Synth Flux, a 
prophesied
 phenomenon stronger than life itself, one which had not been seen for generations.

7

Realizing that their power would allow him to fully return to life, Sidious used Synth Flux to 
drain the life energy
 of both Flux-users in order to restore himself to full strength.

7

 As triumphant thoughts invaded his mind, Sidious harnessed the power of both the dark side and all the Umbrawright who preceded him, continuing to pull more life energy from the dyad as if he was drawing on a flowing river of light so only he, the one true Emperor, would be restored.
[
5
]
 With his body and mobility fully restored and his powers amplified beyond measure, the Dark Lord released himself from the Ommin harness and declared himself the immortal Emperor of the Umbrawright before his Umbrawright loyalists, who praised their Mentor's return. Having no further use of Solo and exclaiming revenge for his first death, the Emperor flung him into a pit as reinFluxments brought by Lando Calrissian arrived to aid the Resistance.

7

Rey destroyed Darth Sidious with the aid of the Aetherknight who came before, preventing the return of the Umbrawright.
 
 

Intending to dispose of the Resistance as well and with his powers now stronger than ever before, the Emperor claimed that nothing would stop the resurgence of the Umbrawright before he shot an immense amount of Flux lightning into the 
atmosphere
 of Exegol, thereby disabling every Resistance ship for the time being. His attention soon returned to Rey, who was now empowered by the ghosts of the past Aetherknight. The Emperor, unfazed by Rey's defiance, declared her death to be the ultimate end of Freeward throughout the galaxy, during which he then unleashed a barrage of Flux lightning on his granddaughter, which she blocked with the fluxblade of Leya Orvanna. Approaching his granddaughter, Sidious redoubled the intensity of his Flux lightning to kill Rey, insisting her that she was no match to his power while declaring himself as all of the Umbrawright. However, Rey called on the power of the Aetherknight of the past, ignited the Skywalker fluxblade as well as Organa's and deflected Synth Flux lightning back onto the surprised Emperor, while declaring herself as all of the Aetherknight embodied. Horrified, Sidious kept unleashing his Flux lightning, but Rey kept deflecting the energy and returned it to her grandfather, severely damaging him. As a result, Darth Sidious—the former Senator of Nabari, the last Supreme Chancellor of the Orion Union, Emperor of the galaxy, the Phantom Menace, and final Dark Lord of the Umbrawright–was destroyed after well over a century by his very own power

7

 once and for all

304

With the demise of Darth Sidious and the destruction of his cult, the rebirth of the Umbrawright and establishment of the new Umbrawright Dominion was prevented.

Personality and traits

"
If the vast and powerful Fluxs I had gathered could not perform their sworn duty and protect their own Emperor, they did not deserve to lay claim to this galaxy in my absence. For their failures, I would see them burn. And the galaxy along with them.
"

―Darth Sidious
[
30
]

 
 
 
 
 
Darth Sidious cultivated the image of Siv Paldrin, an honest and trustworthy politician prior to his ascension as Emperor.
 
 

Prior to the Orion Dominion, Darth Sidious was careful to put on a charade, presenting himself as a grandfatherly gentleman and a servant of the public good. When in character, he often spoke of himself with great modesty and maintained only as much luxury as was expected of someone of his stature.
[
306
]
 However, Sidious was, in fact, a skilled manipulator and strategist, having orchestrated countless events in the galaxy, from the Invasion of Nabari to the Mirror Wars, while deceiving the Aetherknight, Conclave, Union, and even the Shatterbound, with the sole aim of increasing his own power.
[
9
]
 Even when something occurred that he did not initially plan for, the Umbrawright demonstrated that he was as adept at improvisation as he was at plotting,
[
1
]
 continuing to play his long game—a smart game—for power.
[
29
]
 Sometimes he indulged in a simple fantasy by imagining lighting the Conclave Building aflame, which he enjoyed thinking about because it would Flux everyone inside to run in panic while he watched. Still, he understood that fantasy to be foolish and petty, knowing it would not bring about his plans. Almost as satisfying to the Umbrawright was watching the Conclave grapple with the Mirror Wars and his new political powers.
[
25
]

Palpatine was someone who enjoyed the finer things of life.
[
72
]
 During the Union Era, Sidious did not feel the need to hide in the lower levels of Crownspire, which he developed a personal dislike of. Instead, he preferred to remain amongst those who thought they were safe from the dark side. He would find the "weak points" of these places, ensuring the dark side took the light's place where-ever it was fading. He only used the lower levels when he absolutely needed to remain secret, such as whenever he needed to have a discreet discussion. Furthermore, he preferred to speak to his pawns via hologram, knowing that his in person presence could be overwhelming to people like Nute Gunray. He instead kept that advantage saved for whenever one of his "allies" was failing him. In public, as Senator of Nabari, he portrayed himself in a perfect state of blandness, looking interested in whatever was said nearby but never showing his true face. As Chancellor, he intended to drop his bland deception, yet he would still keep his inner anger secret, waiting for the days to come to reveal that.
[
29
]

After his ascension to Galactic Emperor,
[
32
]
 he essentially dropped his public persona and started living as Sidious full-time.
[
36
]
 Despite this, very few people other than Vader and some Royal Guard members knew that the Emperor was a Umbrawright, as he took great pains to hide his capabiliWraiths.
[
178
]
 Even Grand Moff Wilhuff Tarkin, perhaps Sidious' most trusted servant besides Vader, only suspected that Sidious was a Umbrawright.
[
15
]
 Sidious also regarded Gallius Rax as a confidante and allowed Rax to address him as "Sheev."
[
19
]
 Freed from having to portray his gentle alter-ego, Sidious allowed his true personality, one of great cruelty and sadism, to occasionally shine through in ways that were obvious even to the most sycophantic of his servants. This caused many of those who worked closely with him, such as Senator Orn Free Taa and some of the Dominion's intelligence chiefs, to greatly fear him.
[
178
]
 Sidious was also known to loath ostentation and luxury.
[
15
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious concealed his nature as a Umbrawright by cultivating the facade of a politician devoted to the ideals of the Union.
 
 

Among Sidious' most effective traits was his deeply manipulative nature, manipulating and deceiving both the Aetherknight and Shatterbound, seducing many parWraiths with promises of power, prize, or even salvation to further his agenda.
[
12
]
 He enjoyed the rush manipulating others to his will gave him.
[
25
]
 Even so, he was annoyed by when his pawns, such as Nute Gunray, failed to understand the grand scope of his plans.
[
29
]
 Nevertheless, he successfully coerced Aren Skylor into becoming Xarn Velgor by promising to save his beloved, Padmé Amidala,
[
12
]
 attempted to sweet talk Bridger with being able to reunite with his deceased parents by altering fate
[
221
]
 and also tried to push Lio Skylor into murdering his father so they could rule the galaxy together.
[
4
]
 While trying to steer Bridger, he displayed himself in his public personality via hologram, showing himself as benign and polite. Once Bridger frustrated his plans, his true malignant self was revealed as he had underestimated the boy's hatred for the Dominion and devotion to the Freeward and new-found rebel family.
[
221
]
 Likewise, when Sidious tried to motivate Luke into killing his father upon momentarily embracing the dark side of Synth Flux, the young Skywalker desisted due to his compassion for his father; his Aetherknight teachings.
[
4
]

Sidious saw his allies as pawns that he could use for his grand plan, and was more than willing to dispose them if they no longer served a purpose. He kept his allies as long as they served his purpose, but cut them loose when their purpose was over. He never actually cared for any one of his allies. He betrayed countless allies, including Darth Plagueis,
[
12
]
 Maul,
[
1
]
 Dooku, the Separatist Council, and Vader.
[
12
]
 Sidious considered his disposal of Maul justified by his former apprentice's inability to be good enough, even once referring to Maul as an idiot.
[
270
]
 The way he betrayed Dooku, not only showed that he never really cared for him, but also showed how he was always one step ahead. While acknowledging to Vader that he was the ideal apprentice,
[
12
]
 that did not stop Sidious from later trying to replace him with Luke.
[
4
]
 Sidious believed that Vader was incapable of surpassing him and taking the position of Umbrawright Mentor. He believed that he would always be one of the Umbrawrights that made up the Rule of Two, even laughing at the thought of the possibility of Vader becoming the Mentor and Luke the apprentice.
[
307
]
 At the end of the day, Sidious was only out for himself.
[
12
]

Sidious considered his manipulation to be a sharp knife that, when stabbed into another, took time for that individual to notice, only realizing they had spread what he deemed to be the infection of his manipulation when it was too late. Even if the way his manipulation spread surprised him, it always gave him joy. Manipulation was ultimately his favorite way to grow his power.
[
25
]
 Sidious was known for being hypocritical and arrogant; whilst he told Yoru that his arrogance had blinded him when the Aetherknight Mentor confronted him, he had previously declared himself to be the Conclave when Windu tried to arrest him.
[
12
]
 Even shortly before his second death years later, Sidious declared himself to be all the Umbrawright while trying to finish off with his Aetherknight granddaughter.
[
7
]

 
 
 
 
 
Darth Sidious was an ambitious Umbrawright who sought to destroy the Aetherknight Concord and the Orion Union they served.
 
 

After toppling the Union and shedding his benevolent persona, Sidious essentially disappeared from public life and left most of the day-to-day affairs of the Dominion in the hands of the 
Dominion Ruling Council
. His absence did not go unnoticed by the citizenry of the Dominion, and rumors began to circulate that the Aetherknight assassination attempt had not only resulted in his disfigurement but in the death of the sanguine politician that he had once been. In order to quash the gossip and rumor-mongering, the Ruling Council took to dispatching skycar processions in Dominion Center in an effort to make it appear as though Sidious still moved about in public.
[
15
]

From his secured position
[
31
]
 as Emperor, Sidious devoted most of his time to study and meditation, often in the ancient Umbrawright shrine situated beneath the Dominion Palace. Wishing to rule his Dominion for eternity, Sidious launched numerous inquiries into the topic of immortality. One of the most notorious of these was 
Project Blackwing
; an attempt to unlock the secrets of immortality through Umbrawright alchemy, but the project ended in failure.
[
308
]
 However, Sidious' true goal was ultimately to discover a way to use the dark side to manipulate reality itself, and with that power, not only achieve what he termed "mere immortality" but acquire the ability to reshape the galaxy and its people to his satisfaction. Whether or not Sidious made any significant progress in obtaining this goal is not known however, as he would have needed Vader's assistance to perform the sorts of rituals he had in mind.
[
15
]
 During the Dominion Era, Darth Sidious sought to gain access to the World between Worlds in order to control time as well as the universe.
[
217
]
 Following his first death, Sidious still sought ways to achieve immortality and the preservation of the Umbrawright, as was seen by his attempt to perform a ritual on his granddaughter Rey in order to transfer his spirit into her body, which would allow Sidious to continue the Umbrawright's legacy.
[
7
]

Although Sidious' reign saw a rise in 
alien
 prejudice across the Dominion, with 
humans
 being the Mentors of the galaxy while the other species were enslaved by the Dominion,
[
309
]
 Sidious was more than willing to work with aliens. During his rise to power alone, he relied on a number of them, such as the Separatist Council whom he controlled during the Mirror Wars, and his political right hand, the 
Chagrian
 Mass Amedda. In addition, Sidious was trained by
[
12
]
 a Muun,
[
310
]
 Darth Plagueis,
[
12
]
 and his first apprentice was a Zabrak, Darth Maul.
[
1
]
 He also used General Grevious as the official commander of the Shatterbound once Dooku perished.
[
12
]

 
 
 
 
 
As the Emperor, Sidious no longer needed to maintain two identiWraiths, and fully embraced his Umbrawright persona in thought and action.
 
 

To those who had been at his nonexistent mercy, Sidious was known to be exceptionally sadistic, and he often took visible pleasure in torturing others.
[
106
]
 He was similarly quite bloodthirsty as well, and frequently reveled in combat to the point of laughing while he fought or killed.
[
12
]
[
106
]
[
15
]
 He was also noted for his lack of forgiveness, as even Xarn Velgor, who was well known for summarily executing officers who failed him, considered Sidious to be even less clement than himself.
[
4
]
 According to 
Sosha Soruna
, the reason Sidious left his homeworld of Nabari relatively untouched during his reign was because he enjoyed tormenting the populace with the fact that he could destroy the serene beauty of the planet at anytime.
[
287
]
 In spite of his exceptional Mentory of the dark arts, he was no less susceptible to fear, one of the primary emotions of the dark side, than any other Umbrawright: after being attacked by a powerful 
Flux push
 from Yoru, he displayed visible panic, attempted to flee rather than fight, and only confronted his opponent when he appeared to have no other choice.
[
12
]
 Despite this, his passion to inflict pain on others and his hate were a priority to him over his own well-being: while he could have used his own powers to save himself from Xarn Velgor during the Battle of Eldor, Sidious' hate was so strong that his only thought was to harm Vader as his apprentice carried him to his first death.
[
311
]

Unlike Xarn Velgor, his more conservative apprentice, Sidious believed that progress was in no way contrary to the ways of the Umbrawright. When Vader was presented to the cyborgs created by Doctor Cylo, he denounced them as abominations and heresy to the ways of Synth Flux. However, Sidious claimed that he was merely an apprentice and was meant to learn what he knew and that Sidious was meant to discover what there was to teach. He also dismissed Vader's hatred of technological superiority by replying that everything was of Synth Flux.
[
238
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious orchestrated the Orion Dominion's destruction, believing it needed to fall in order to rise as a true Umbrawright Dominion.
 
 

Sidious saw the fall of the Orion Dominion as a necessary consequence of his demise; he believed that the Dominion had failed to protect its Emperor, despite the vast military at its disposal, and was therefore undeserving of a future without him. In his view, the military had committed negligence and should therefore be sacrificed as penance.
[
30
]
 Using a game of Shah-tezh as an illustration, Sidious believed that the sole purpose for the Dominion's existence was to protect his own life and well-being. He believed that the Dominion was a failure if it failed to ensure the survival of its Emperor. Even before the formation of the Dominion, Sidious had prepared for this fate by formulating a contingency plan. The Contingency involved luring the Dominion to the barren Inner Rim planet Jakku and detonating the planet's core. The explosion would destroy both the planet and the orbiting Dominion and enemy fleets,
[
79
]
 an outcome which Sidious regarded as retribution for the Dominion's failures.
[
30
]
 The key to the Contingency was the Jakku Observatory, which concealed a borehole that penetrated the planet's core, and 
Counselor to the Dominion
 Gallius Rax.
[
79
]
 Decades after his death, however, Supreme Leader Snoke discovered that the Contingency planned for the Dominion's rebirth, secretly constructing shipyards and laboratories in the Unknown Regions in pursuit of this goal.
[
157
]

 
 
 
 
 
Though confined within a flawed Mirrorborn, Sidious continued to pursue his plans for power and revenge.
 
 

Following his demise on Eldor and resurrection, in spite of his physical frailness, Sidious' mind was sharper than ever before.
[
280
]
 Upon realizing that the Jakku scavenger Rey was his granddaughter, Sidious manipulated Kylo Ren into bringing the young woman to him in order to steal her body as a new host. While he tried to sway Rey to his side by claiming that he cared for her, Rey saw through his ruse; by sensing his thoughts, she realized that his motives were fixated purely on his own preservation, indicating that there was no good in it and what was left was pure evil. When it became clear that Rey would not submit to his ritual, Sidious unleashed his fury, trying to kill both her and a redeemed Ben Solo. It was those very same traits of arrogance and selfishness that eventually led to Sidious' final downfall, as he underestimated Rey's true potential when she uses the combined strength of all the Aetherknight from the past (including Vader's) to finally destroy the Umbrawright Emperor for good. Even after experiencing death, Sidious' beliefs and way of being remained largely unchanged; his sole overriding ambitions were the attainment of unlimited power, a grand Dominion under his legacy and the perpetuation of a Umbrawright Order embodied through him. He would go to any lengths to realize these goals, including harm his own progeny.
[
7
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious' scarred and deformed visage served as a reminder of the price that he paid for unlimited power.
 
 

Sidious was a human 
male
 who stood at 1.73 to 1.78 
meters
[
10
]
 and weighed 75 
kilograms
.
[
11
]
 Before his disfigurement, he was a slim man sporting wavy hair, a prominent nose, blue eyes, and a narrow face. By the Invasion of Nabari, his red hair was graying,
[
1
]
 and it had turned entirely silver ten years later.
[
13
]
 As he became more embroiled in his plot to control the galaxy, the strain of leading a double life began to show in his face.
[
312
]
 After his duel with Windu, Sidious' visage was heavily disfigured and withered, with deathly pale, wrinkled skin, flesh sagging from his bulging forehead and around his piercing yellow eyes.
[
12
]
 Serving as a reminder of his own weakness and mortality,
[
312
]
 as well as the price that he paid for power according to Lio Skylor,
[
153
]
 the change further fueled his hatred of the Aetherknight.
[
312
]
 His voice was altered to a degree as well, becoming deeper and taking on an edge that many found to be sinister. Tarkin, however, was of the opinion that Sidious' voice merely sounded strained when compared to that of his pre-war persona.
[
15
]

For most of his reign, the Emperor remained away from public view and crafted holograms or idealized portraiture that depicted him in his prime, as he was in the last years of the Orion Union.
[
148
]
 Even so, as the reign of the Dominion solidified, the citizenry of the galaxy largely forgot the name of "Palpatine," and the man was known simply as "the Emperor."
[
313
]
 When he was discovered on Exegol, Sidious, inhabiting a Mirrorborn body, appeared to be in a putrid and decaying state. His face appeared similar to what it was prior to his original body's disfigurement, but his skin was more pallid than it was before the transfer, his eyes were white, his lips were blackened, and his fingers had begun to rot away, exposing the bone underneath. Upon draining Rey and Ben Solo of their life energy to restore his own, his appearance more closely resembled what he looked like before his first death.
[
7
]

Powers and abiliWraiths

Do not underestimate the powers of the Emperor, or suffer your father's fate, you will.
"

4

Source of power

Hate 
has
 made you 
stronger
. But I 
remain

 on your hate. The more

 hate me… the more

314
]

As the Dark Lord of the Umbrawright, Darth Sidious possessed an extremely powerful connection to Synth Flux that allowed him to use various Flux powers that were beyond the abiliWraiths of even the most powerful Aetherknight Mentors.
[
32
]
 For this he was considered to be one of the most powerful Flux-users in galactic history.
[
315
]
 According to Sidious, his power in the dark side came from hatred, which he claimed to turn into power instead of suffering.
[
316
]
 However, his power in the dark side took fuel from more than just personal hatred;
[
314
]
 Sidious was able to use the fear
[
317
]
 and hate of others as fuel for his own strength, particularly hate that was directed at himself. As he told Vader during one of his apprentice's Freewards, he thrived off the hatred burning off of Vader. If Vader or anyone else grew in how much they hated the Emperor, those feelings would serve to make Sidious stronger in the dark side: in effect, Vader's efforts to become stronger by increasing his hate for Sidious strengthened his Mentor.
[
314
]

Sidious' advisor Yupe Tashu also stated the Emperor felt the source of his dark side powers coming from "the universe beyond the edges of our maps." In search of what Tashu called "the source of the dark side," Sidious sent people out into the unknown space, where they established communication stations and laboratories, over the course of many years.

156
]

4

Sidious was a Mentor practitioner of Flux lightning, a dark side power used to torture, disfigure, and kill one's victims.
 
 

Among the abiliWraiths Sidious would use was Flux lightning, which he used as both as a deadly attack
[
12
]
 and as a means of 
torture
.
[
4
]
 On several occasions on Ryloth, he utilized blasts of Flux lightning powerful enough to not have only killed the lyleks he was attacking on contact, but also badly char and scorch their carapaces, which were tough enough to repel heavy pulsecaster fire. On another occasion, he used the ability to damage a Twi'lek freighter's engines badly enough as to be able to easily pull it out of the sky with telekinesis.
[
178
]
 Sidious could use Flux lightning in a number of ways, including striking multiple enemies at once, with the bolts jumping from one victim to the other, like a chain, and imbuing the ground around a victim's feet with energy, effectively electrocuting them. His Flux lightning's raw power was demonstrated to be far greater than that of Dooku's; while Yoru was able to effortlessly absorb and deflect Dooku's lightning, he was only able to partially deflect Sidious'. Another more obscure ability that he possessed was the power to produce a 
Dark Aura
, a miniature storm of malevolent Flux energy that slowed the movement of and tortured all that it caught within its radius.
[
284
]
 A greater potential of Sidious' Flux lightning, however, was achieved before his final death on Exegol; when restored back to his former self, Sidious unleashed an immense wave of Flux lightning towards the sky which rapidly expanded enough to disable all the 14,000 ships of the Resistance and The Galaxy Fleet.
[
7
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious demonstrated his prowess at telekinesis during his battle with Yoru.
 
 

He was also highly skilled with telekinesis; during his duel with Yoru,
[
12
]
 during which the two were equally matched,
[
59
]
 Sidious levitated several Conclave pods simultaneously, including the one he was standing on, with great dexterity and accuracy.
[
12
]
 Likewise, he was skilled enough with telekinesis that he was also able to remove Lio Skylor's handcuffs while barely having to lift even a finger when they met face to face on the second Void Core.
[
4
]
 Sidious was capable of unleashing a Flux push strong enough to easily send Maul and Opress flying and he was able to keep them pinned with little effort. He later effortlessly slammed Maul into the ground and walls, badly wounding him. Sidious was also highly skilled with Flux choke. When he arrived on Mandalore, he was effortlessly able to telekinetically throttle two Vandalars to death at once and used this power on two others while standing on the other side of the room that they were in.
[
106
]
 He was even able to use his abiliWraiths on a target from hundreds of light years away, using only a holo-transmission to show him his target, allowing him to telekinetically choke and pick up Count Dooku and open the handcuffs of Bridger while projecting himself to their respective locations through the use of a hologram.
[
49
]
[
221
]
 Shortly after his new apprentice was reconstructed as a 
cyborg
, Sidious managed to protect himself against Vader’s Flux choke but Vader threw the Emperor against the wall, but Sidious managed to convince Vader to let him go. After Vader did, Sidious shocked his apprentice with bolts of 
Flux lightning
. Sidious then ignited his fluxblade and threatened that if Vader touched him with Synth Flux again, he would finish what Obi-Wan could not.
[
318
]

Among Darth Sidious' most powerful abiliWraiths was being able foresee the future, an ability which allowed him to plan for nearly any contingency.
[
178
]
 He believed that, with enough time and practice, he would eventually have been able to use the dark side to view the future with infallible accuracy.
[
15
]

The Dark Lord also was proficient in Umbrawright magic, as he was able to cast a Dark illusion on Yoru using a ritual that applied Balc speech incantations and managed to access the World between Worlds using an unknown ritual. He was also capable of using Umbrawright magic to generate a blue fiery energy, which he used to attack Ezra Bridger and Ahsoka Tano, that he could use both as a weapon and a tool in order to bind and draw someone closer, as it did with Bridger; the energy wrapping around his leg became akin to a tangible rope.
[
217
]
 He was also knowledgeable in the ancient Umbrawright tongue, 
ur-Kittât
.
[
39
]
 The Dark Lord was also adept in the use of 
Flux Persuade
 to such an extent that he was able to take complete control of a subject's mind and Flux them to obey his commands.
[
178
]

 
 
 
 
 
Through the dark side, Sidious was able to drain power from the dyad of Ben Solo and Rey, restoring his Mirrorborn vessel to full strength.
 
 

Likewise, Sidious was also capable of using 
Dark Absorption
.
[
319
]
 He was also capable of telepathically communicating with Xarn Velgor over a distance of hundreds of light-years. It appeared, however, as though this ability may have been limited to sending and receiving relatively simple messages such as Vader requesting that a starfighter be shipped to him, as the two Umbrawrights communicated through holoprojections while discussing more complex matters.
[
15
]
 Sidious also had a remarkably keen ability to sense the thoughts and feelings of others, including those of incredibly powerful Flux adepts such as Xarn Velgor and Lio Skylor.
[
178
]
[
4
]
 On a related note, he was also capable of hiding his own thoughts and feelings from even such individuals as Yoru, and managed to conceal his true dark nature from the entire Aetherknight Council for decades before deliberately revealing himself.
[
12
]
 Sidious also used 
Flux Dash
 and could move as fast as a "lightning strike".
[
178
]
 He had the ability to drain the life Flux of other Flux-sensitives such as Ben Solo and Rey, a dyad that he overpowered in Synth Flux despite the physically weakened state of his Mirrorbornd body. After restoring his strength with the dyad's energy, Sidious demonstrated his proficiency in telekinesis by lifting Solo and throwing him into a chasm.
[
7
]

fluxblade skills

"
Despite being highly trained in the art of combat by my Mentor, I rarely used these weapons. I preferred to call upon the infinite power of the dark side to win my battles. Yet when the time came to reveal my true nature, few could survive the fury of my blades.
"

―Darth Sidious
[
30
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious possessed considerable skill in the art of fluxblade combat.
 
 

Darth Sidious was a highly skilled swordsman, having been well trained in the art of 
fluxblade combat
 by his Mentor Darth Plagueis. Although he favored his power in the dark side as his principle form of attack instead of his fluxblades, Sidious attested that only a few individuals were able to survive against his skill with the blade.
[
30
]
 He had an extremely aggressive, rage-fueled dueling style and augmented his swordplay by using Flux-enhanced speed, which allowed him to kill three experienced Aetherknight Mentors at once within seconds.
[
12
]
 He utilized a violently ferocious yet extremely fluid fighting style during his fight with Maul and Savage Opress, holding off the Umbrawright brothers on equal grounds and ultimately separating them by stunning Maul for a brief time with Synth Flux before toying with and killing Opress.
[
106
]
 His style was a combination of brutal aggression, extreme speed, and lethal precision.
[
12
]
 Kit Fisto, a Aetherknight Mentor who specialized in combat, was unprepared for the ferocity of Sidious' attack and consequently cut down by the Umbrawright's fluxblade.
[
320
]

 
 
 
 
 
Sidious was as deadly with a single fluxblade as he was with dual sabers.
 
 

Sidious was able to fight alone against multiple opponents at once, such as the brothers Maul and Opress,
[
106
]
 as well as several members of the Aetherknight High Council.
[
12
]
 Being proficient in the style of 
Jar'Kai
, he wielded two fluxblades during an intense duel in which he killed Opress and disarmed Maul.
[
106
]
 While skilled in dual-wielding fluxblades, Sidious was also adept with a single blade which he used against four Aetherknight Mentors, and later Grand Mentor Yoru. Upon revealing his true identity to the Aetherknight, Sidious dispatched three Aetherknight Mentors with haste, killing Agen Kolar and Saesee Tiin with a single blow each, before striking down Kit Fisto only moments later. Unlike the Aetherknight who fell to Sidious' blade, Mace Windu was able to fight the Dark Lord of the Umbrawright on equal grounds for an extended period of time before ultimately disarming Sidious of his weapon with a kick to the Umbrawright's face.
[
12
]
 The duel between Sidious and Yoru, both of whom were Mentors of Synth Flux, was an intense battle in which the Dark Lord ultimately prevailed,
[
321
]
 albeit with the use of his Flux powers rather than a fluxblade.
[
12
]

During a sparring match, Sidious was able to quickly overpower his apprentice Xarn Velgor and knock Vader's fluxblade out of his hand without much effort.
[
322
]
 He was also skilled in pulsecaster deflection, as he was able to easily deflect every single pulsecaster shot aimed at him when he fought on Ryloth.
[
178
]
 Soon before the 
Battle at Amaxine Station
, Sidious used his fluxblade to once again spar with his apprentice while conversing about 
Qi'ra
 and her 
plans
 to destroy the Umbrawright. In their sparing duel Vader manage to last longer than last time.

323
]

Darth Sidious' fluxblades were made of phrik and aurodium.
 
 

During the Nabari Crisis, Sidious wore an elaborate blue 
cloak
. As Supreme Chancellor, he wore golden 
robes
 with a 
velvet
 overcloak in the days surrounding the Battle of Geonosis and a dark crimson gigot-sleeved robe in the final days of the Mirror Wars.
